## CPF-FR-007931

- **Work Item / Priority:** `CPF-WP-OPS-CAPACITY-05-OPERATIONS_SECURITY` / `P1`
- **Requirement / Acceptance:** [OPS-CAPACITY / 기능 목적·업무 결과] CPU/memory/thread/connection/queue/storage/DB/Broker 용량·threshold·trend·forecast·load test 기준을 제공한다. 이를 위해 해결할 업무 문제, 사용 역할, 미제공 위험과 완료 결과를 정의한다. 선언·Interface·DTO·문서만 추가하지 말고 실제 Consumer와 전체 호출 경로를 구현한다. | 플랫폼 운영자·SRE가 실제 제품 경로에서 기능 목적·업무 결과 기능을 사용할 때 CPU/memory/thread/connection/queue/storage/DB/Broker 용량·threshold·trend·forecast·load test 기준을 제공한다.의 결과와 상태·권한·Audit·복구 규칙이 일치해야 한다. - **Source / Consumer / Full call path:** cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmHealthController.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmSystemInfoController.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmObservabilityController.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/service/AdmObservabilityService.java; runtime state providers | ADM capacity/health diagnostics and runtime operators | Runtime resource/state metrics -> observability service -> capacity query/detail -> operator decision/runbook
- **Behavior / Test / Assertion:** Capacity metrics include scope, time, units and saturation signals. | Missing/stale/over-limit data is explicit and paged safely. | Refresh/retry and historical comparison support action. | ADM health/observability tests; runtime state tests; load/performance environment pending | exact source SHA·clean tree·command·environment·start/end·exit code·actual output·report/artifact SHA-256·sanitization | CPF-SC-019204 DIRECT_VERIFICATION: the linked requirement acceptance must be directly traced to source, consumer, assertion and evidence; expected=플랫폼 운영자·SRE가 실제 제품 경로에서 API·OpenAPI 기능을 사용할 때 CPU/memory/thread/connection/queue/storage/DB/Broker 용량·threshold·trend·forecast·load test 기준을 제공한다.의 결과와 상태·권한·Audit·복구 규칙이 일치해야 한다. - **Command / Exit / Actual:** `GitHub Connector exact-SHA source/consumer inventory + local CSV contract reconstruction` / `0 (source/ledger reconstruction); target tools separated` / CPF-SC-019204 개별 판정: 재확인 필요/재확인 필요; DIRECT_VERIFICATION Acceptance·Steps·Expected·Failure Criteria·Assertion을 전용 anchor에 기록; 직접 Harness 또는 exact-SHA source reconstruction 근거, 목표환경 차이는 재확인 필요
